# Approved Wave3 requirement-to-evidence matrix — 2026-10-02

All95FR,26AC,40EC and20NFR IDs/full approved wording are retained in the companion JSON. This matrix separates confirmed development gaps, missing verification and external acceptance. Wave3 is not fully complete.

HEAD f9cc342815e2446fb3f26204d5e65e03c1b2a140; staged paths 0. Current whole desktop 1179 passes/85 failures. Whole platform 977 passes/8 failures. Failure ownership is recorded in the cited logs; whole-checkout results can include concurrent Wave4 changes. Focused runs overlap and are not added to whole-suite totals.

Reviewed pure-domain scope 643/677 branches (94.98%); changed whole-file Dart 15310/19131 lines (80.03%). Source mismatches 12; unmeasured executable files 12. The numerical gate is open. SQL assertions are not SQL branch coverage.

|ID|Approved behavior|Reviewed implementation sources|Verification evidence|Missing implementation|Missing verification|External acceptance|
